# SOMA — THREAD実装マッピング

> SOMAアーキテクチャのTHREADプロジェクトへの具体的実装対応。
> 理論定義は [[SOMA]]（sublimate-vault）を参照。

## Goals (SSOT — migrated from GOALS.md 2026-03-17)

Quality-first. No deadline.

### L1: Isaac Lab Simulation Full Pipeline

**Scope correction (2026-06-05):** Human-Rs rescoped the current project target to
Isaac Lab / SIM. Real-world physical Franka × 2 deployment is **not** part of
the current project scope. REAL2SIM / sim-to-real transfer remains a future
consideration only, not a current deliverable. The final / ultimate target
remains **100%** (Rs 2026-06-23 に100%へ定性的に再定義; 数値記載だが現時点では**定性的目標**として解釈 = 完全動作を目指す aspirational target、literal 統計 SR の測定対象ではない; = the P0-KILL #2 predicate-redefinition), but the current bar is basic SIM operation first, even rough or
imperfect; precision and accuracy improvement come after basic operation works.
Human-Rs also qualified that the foundation must remain rigorous: robot
mechanism, environment, and cable are the basis of everything and must be
physically valid, reuse-first, and gate-reviewed. The rough/imperfect latitude
applies only to task performance / routing success / precision, not to the
foundation. Spring-follow, `KINEMATIC_INV_MASS`, kinematic tricks, teleport, or
physics-bypass holds are not acceptable foundation evidence merely because the
target is SIM. Cable model wording must stay source-faithful: installed Newton
`add_rod` evidence is rigid capsules with `CABLE` joints, not a particle
Cosserat-rod premise unless a later reviewed source establishes otherwise.

**Deploy cable-state scope (Rs 2026-06-24, recorded 2026-06-25 — SUPERSEDES the earlier "deploy = cable FIXED position" framing; the old `task_config.py` "SOMA L38" deploy-fixed distinction no longer holds and is annotated SUPERSEDED at `task_config.py:257-262`):** the cable TYPE is fixed, but at deploy the cable **POSITION + POSE vary RANDOMLY** (Rs「位置姿勢はランダムに対応する必要あり」) **AND SHAPE varies via bending / 屈曲**. → handling random cable position/pose/shape is now a **DEPLOY requirement**, not demo-only augmentation. Initial DR envelope (Rs-confirmed 2026-06-25, may expand per actual deploy need): **position ±20mm / yaw-Z ±12° / Z ±5mm** (all within demonstrated open-loop grasp tolerances). SHAPE: vertical SAG is conservatively COVERED for the both-arms-lift pipeline (Stage B, %2 cross-PV CONCUR); horizontal routing curvature = **KINEMATIC** (the env7 cable is vertical-sag-only — Rs DECISION B2, `RS71-System-Spec-SSOT.md` §4). The straight fixed-IC grasp banking (held-rate / IC-robustness / yaw-Z) is a **mechanism-precision FLOOR**, NON-conservative w.r.t. this deploy state-space. The L3 DR-wiring track (apply the envelope to the CLAMP-env reset DR + policy-retrain) = a SEPARATE parallel effort to scope. Authoritative cross-refs: `RS71-System-Spec-SSOT.md` §0 INVARIANT 4 + `task_config.py:257-264`; node `eval_runs/troot_clamp_cable_state_space_20260624/PROPOSAL.md`.

| Criterion | Threshold |
|-----------|-----------|
| Grasp method | SIM-side grasp/control sufficient for initial task operation |
| Pipeline | SIM Grasp → Lift (50mm target; **live datum = コ ~45mm level**, BANKED CPU building-block 2026-06-21, Rs source-GO — GPU/full-route-snag-retention/R2/R3 = production-pending) → Route (131mm) → Hook placement — pipeline-detail SSOT = `RS71-System-Spec-SSOT.md` §6. ✅ **FORM-vs-LIFT decision RESOLVED 2026-06-20** (human chose コ-shape finger; the ◇ V-groove open-bottom that could NOT clamp+lift is DISCARDED as a design reference) — lift mechanism is no longer open; datum SSOT = `RS71-System-Spec-SSOT.md` §6:66 + §5 + §0 INVARIANT 4 |
| cable_on_hook | true in Isaac Lab / SIM (cable_z > 0.78 diagnostic retained) |
| Foundation | Rigorous robot mechanism + environment + cable; no spring-follow/kinematic/teleport/physics-bypass foundation shortcut |
| Kinematic teleport (Fix 1) | NOT used |
| Reproducibility | Current bar: operating at all in SIM; final bar: improve toward **100%** (Rs 2026-06-23 に100%へ定性的に再定義, 現時点では定性的目標; L2 criteria の heuristic ≥95% は別 metric で保持) |
| NaN | 0 |

### L2: SOMA-Compliant Architecture (on top of L1)

| Criterion | Threshold |
|-----------|-----------|
| Code structure | `soma/` directory with 8 SOMA components |
| Integration loop | THREADEnv.step() with SOMA structural flow (Predictor/Arbiter: R2-deferred stubs) |
| Safety Envelope | 4-layer enforcement (Energy Tank / Passivity / CBF-QP / Adaptive Retreat) |
| Fix 2 absorption | Joint Vel Clamping → CBF h_joint_vel constraint |
| Heuristic under Safety | ≥ 95% success rate with Safety Envelope enforced |
| Sensory Front-End | soma/sensory_frontend.py with 24D obs builder + Vision Stage 1-3 integration |
| NC/PS Interface | Type, range, cutoff, fallback validation active |

### Out of Scope (not part of L2)

- Predictor 3-channel (v2-C) — trigger: heuristic failure on new task
- Arbiter blend transition (v2-D) — trigger: force spike issue on multi-phase task
- Modulator / Residual PPO — trigger: heuristic failure
- REAL2SIM / sim-to-real transfer as current deliverable (future consideration only)
- Physical Franka × 2 deployment, lab-resource acquisition, and real-robot safety/ops for this project scope
- Non-rigorous robot/environment/cable foundation; spring-follow, kinematic inverse-mass support, teleport, or physics-bypass hold as foundation proof
- Multiple hooks, disturbance rejection
- N>1 parallelization

| B3 | action空間定義 | [-1,1]→物理単位の変換表 |
| B4 | フォールバック条件定義 | obs異常→residual=0 |

| Step | 内容 | 検証予測 |
|---|---|---|
| C1 | 18D訓練 | — |
| C2 | 22D訓練 | — |
| C3 | 24D訓練 | — |

### Phase D: 変調器投入（Modulator = Residual PPO）

| Step | 内容 | 検証予測 | 結果 |
|---|---|---|---|
| D1 | ResidualGraspEnv + train_residual_ppo.py | — | ✅ 実装完了 |
| D2 | PPO訓練ループ動作確認（D2.5） | — | ✅ 10/10 iter, 0 fallback |
| D3 | heuristic baseline計測 | — | ✅ 20/20=100%, cable_z=10.04mm±0.02mm |
| D4 | Residual PPO本格訓練 | — | v1 NaN crash (it~125), v2完走 (50K steps) |
| D5 | heuristic vs Residual比較 | P0.3 | ❌ residual 0/8=0% vs heuristic 100% |

**Phase D判定: 条件付きクローズ**
- D3 PASS: heuristic baseline 100%成功、cable_z_delta=10.04mm、173 steps/ep、reward=-1092.97
- D4: v1 NaN crash (v_loss爆発, lr=3e-4)。v2完走 (lr=1e-4, grad_norm=0.5, reward_scale=0.1)
- D5 FAIL: residual+heuristic 0/8成功、cable_z=0.80mm。residualがheuristicを悪化
- **R2検証成功**: 工学解が100%動作する領域に学習投入は有害
- **再導入条件**: heuristicが失敗するタスク出現時（e.g., cable変形大、未知形状グリップ）

- hard switch → ブレンド窓への移行
- 検証予測: P5.1（force spike低減）

- Transition予測モデルの追加

## 観測空間（Sensory Front-End実装）

### 24D obs構成（Phase A3で確定予定）

| 次元 | 変数 | 型 | ソース |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0-6 | left_joint_pos (7) | proprioceptive | joint state |
| 7-13 | right_joint_pos (7) | proprioceptive | joint state |
| 14-16 | cable_midpoint_pos (3) | visual (E系) | sim state |
| 17 | cable_tension (1) | tactile (F系) | force sensor |
| 18-19 | ee_pos_error_L/R (2) | proprioceptive | computed |
| 20-21 | force_derivative_L/R (2) | tactile (F系) | computed |
| 22-23 | grip_effort_L/R (2) | tactile (F系) | Isaac Sim API（要検証） |

### 22D fallback（grip_effort取得不可時）
- 上記から grip_effort_L/R (2) を除外

## Safety Envelope 4層実装計画

| 層 | SOMA定義 | THREAD実装 | Phase | 状態 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Energy Tank | エネルギー収支カウンタ → 超過時action=0 | B | 🔧 計測のみ（Phase 1） |
| 2 | Passivity Filter | 出力パワー ≤ 散逸パワー制約 | B | 🔧 計測のみ（Phase 1） |
| 3 | CBF | 関節限界・接触力上限のbarrier function | B | 🔧 計測のみ（Phase 1） |
| 4 | Adaptive Retreat | obs異常スコア → heuristic fallback | B/D | ✅ FallbackGuard |

現状: 🔧 Phase 1（計測インフラ整備）— `safety_envelope.py` で4層構造化。層1-3は計測のみ（pass-through）、層4はFallbackGuardが実行時制約を担当。`safety_envelope_metrics.json` に計測データ出力。

## NC/PS Interface（旧 Core/Shell Interface、旧 Learning Boundary）

### インターフェース5原則
1. 型制約: NC出力の型はNC/PS Interfaceが定義
2. 範囲制約: NC出力はPSが定めた安全上下界内
3. 遮断権: PS→NC（身体が知性を制約）
4. フォールバック: NC遮断時のデフォルト値がPSに常備
5. 抽象化: NC→PS間の観測・指令は標準形式（身体固有詳細を隠蔽）

### コンポーネント別インターフェース

| コンポーネント | Shell出力 | 型 | 範囲 | 遮断条件 | フォールバック |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Sensory FE (Stage 1) | seg_mask | bool[H×W] | MAX_PIXELS | Stage 3非物理的 | 前フレーム |
| Predictor | x̂, σ² | float | — | σ² > θ | residual=0 |
| Arbiter | w_select/suppress | float[0,1] | 正規化 | 安全退避 | S1 |
| Modulator | raw_residual | float[-1,1] | δ_max×α | α=0 | zeros |

### 既存のLearning Boundary定義（継承）

| 制約種別 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| Action空間 | [-1,1] normalized → p95 clip後の物理単位 |
| Phase限定 | Residual PPOはPhase 3-5のみ有効 |
| Config freeze | Analytical IKパラメータ、Safety Envelopeパラメータは変更不可 |
| Fallback | Predictor trust region外 → residual = 0 |

旧DC/SSの詳細仕様・検証テスト（SEP-1〜7）は [[Deterministic Core - Stochastic Shell]] を参照。

## T-ROOT R2-A Phase 1（2026-05-20）

R2-A は P0 KILL 後の runner-local observability / contact-presentation 調査。目的は 5-clip 95% 達成 claim ではなく、現 substrate での cable_drop 構造を分解し、次に構造化すべき PS 側不安定性を特定すること。

### Phase 1-A: left EE zero-action retention smoke

| Arm | high_drop cable_drop | Delta vs control | Verdict |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| control | 0.6458 | — | baseline |
| retention_supervised | 0.6667 | +0.021 | NO_GO |

結果: 左 EE action を `actions[:, 6:12] = 0` にする runner-local retention は high_drop を改善せず、混合群のみ部分改善。指 action supervise 仮説は後続 audit で撤回（`NewtonAerialRegraspEnv` は 12D EE action のみで finger command 次元なし）。

### Phase 1-B: contact / presentation smoke

| Candidate | high_drop cable_drop | Delta vs control | Verdict | Note |
|---|---:|---:|---|---|
| control | 0.7292 | — | baseline | same-run control |
| right_approach_damped | 0.7014 | -0.028 | NO_GO | gate barely fired; right arm does not reach 50mm before drop/explosion |
| left_anchor_frozen | 0.5903 | -0.139 | NO_GO | strongest runner-local signal, but below required -0.20 threshold |

Structural finding:
- `no_hold_no_clamp_drop_rate == cable_drop_rate` for all Phase 1-B arms.
- Cable drops occur before either arm establishes the terminal contact predicate.
- Runner-local intervention ceiling observed so far is approximately -14pp high_drop reduction.
- Remaining high_drop ≈59% after left-anchor freeze is treated as cable-physics-level residue, not just EE drift.

### Source-level promotion

Rs selected source-level promotion of the strongest runner-local signal:

- `NewtonAerialRegraspEnv(..., freeze_left_anchor=True)` default ON.
- Per-step freeze sets left deltas to zero and restores `ee_target_left` / `ee_quat_left` to settled cache pose.
- Demo collection scripts explicitly pass `freeze_left_anchor=False` to avoid BC/demo distribution mismatch.
- `task_config.py` remains unchanged.

Current gate state:
- Source-level change executed.
- GPU verification smoke is HIGH-COST-GATE and remains HOLD until explicit Rs/Rs代行 directive.
- This promotion is not a product-predicate success claim; it records a partial PS stabilization with NO_GO smoke evidence.

## T-ROOT R2-A Phase 2（2026-05-21）

Phase 2 は Phase 1 の structural ceiling を受けた cable/support timing diagnostic。目的は product predicate 達成ではなく、drop が contact predicate 前に起きているか、runner-local support がどの failure class に効くかを切り分けること。

### Cable physics timing diagnostic

| Item | Finding |
|---|---|
| Run | `eval_runs/r2a_phase2_cable_physics_diagnostic_20260520/` |
| Status | COMPLETE |
| Dominant failure | pre-contact cable/support failure |
| Key signature | `no_hold_no_clamp_drop_rate == cable_drop_rate` |
| Timing | high-drop failures are early-heavy; many occur by step 20 |
| Interpretation | right arm is too far from terminal contact when cable/support failure occurs |

Conclusion: Phase 1 interventions were limited by a timing/substrate problem before stable left hold / right clamp can be established. This is not chain SR validation and does not change L1 product status.

### Candidate 1: pre-policy support settle

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Run | `eval_runs/r2a_phase2_settle_smoke_20260520/` |
| Status | ABORTED / FAIL |
| Hard guard | midpoint explosion rate >= 0.50 |
| Abort point | 615 / 1107 completions |
| Control explosion at abort | 0.5732 |
| Matched seed0 high_drop | `pre_policy_settle=0.6875` |
| Delta vs source_default | 0.0000 |

Decision: Candidate 1 is not promoted. It did not reduce high_drop and retained the early-heavy failure signature while explosion stayed high.

### Candidate 2: kinematic fixture support

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Design review | `eval_runs/r2a_phase2_candidate2_design_20260521/` |
| Technical feasibility | runner-local implementation feasible |
| Mechanism | pre-contact left-finger kinematic support, release on right proximity or step guard |
| Caveat | VBD `inv_mass=0` kinematic bodies do not provide valid finger-cable friction for physical grasp validation |
| Current smoke | `eval_runs/r2a_phase2_candidate2_kinematic_support_smoke_20260521/` |
| Status | COMPLETE, 1107 / 1107 completions, no abort |
| Smoke verdict | `FAIL_FIXTURE_DIAGNOSTIC` |
| high_drop delta vs source_default | -0.1389 (source_default 0.6319 → candidate 0.4931) |
| GO criterion | FAIL; required delta <= -0.1500, missed by 0.0111 |
| Release addendum | high_drop and mixed records released at step 0 by `right_pos_gate`, so sustained pre-contact support was not tested |

Interpretation rule: Candidate 2 is only a non-physical fixture diagnostic for support/timing/state-sync sensitivity. It must not be used as product success, physical grasp proof, source-level kinematic promotion authorization, or chain-context SR evidence. The physical next path should preserve dynamic/frictional contact, e.g. Candidate 2b.

## 業界ベンチマーク（2026-03-11 調査）

22社/機関のSOMAマッピング結果からTHREADに適用可能な具体技術を抽出。

### THREADに直結する技術

| 技術 | 出典 | 適用先 | 優先度 | 根拠 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **GelSight/GelSlim触覚** | GelSight/MIT CSAIL | ケーブル滑り検出・接触力推定 | **高** | MIT cable manipulation実績。grip-load coupling (F系) 強化 |
| **HOCBF安全保証** | MIT CSAIL | Safety Envelope層3 (CBF) | **高** | 柔軟物体接触の力上限を数学的に保証。Phase B実装候補 |
| **DPPO残差学習** | TRI | Modulator (Phase D) | **高** | OSC保存+拡散ポリシー残差。THREADのAnalytical IK+Residual PPOと同構造 |
| **V-JEPA潜在空間予測** | Meta FAIR | Predictor trust region拡張 | 中 | 自己教師で<62h unlabeledデータからzero-shot。Phase F候補 |
| **Eureka報酬自動設計** | NVIDIA | Modulator報酬関数最適化 | 中 | LLMが報酬関数コード生成→RL訓練→反映。Phase D報酬設計効率化 |
| **OCS2スイッチドシステムMPC** | ETH RSL | Arbiter (Phase E) | 低 | パワー整合切替のMPC実装参考 |
| **Sparsh汎用触覚エンコーダ** | Meta FAIR | Sensory Front-End (F系) | 低 | 触覚SSL。Isaac Sim触覚データ不足時の転移学習候補 |

| Constraint Graph | Phase FSM (7 phases) ✅ | 業界に形式的制約グラフなし | THREADが先行 | — |
| Sensory Front-End | 24D obs (視覚+触覚+固有受容) | Meta DIGIT360 (18+モード) | 触覚解像度 | GelSlim検討 (Phase A拡張) |
| Predictor | force_derivative + ee_error | ETH RSL actuator net + MPC | 世界モデル未導入 | V-JEPA参考 (Phase F) |
| Structure Library | Analytical IK + Force Servo | ETH RSL OCS2 + Drake | 対等 | — |
| Arbiter | hard switch | GR00T N1 dual-system | ブレンド窓未実装 | Phase E で対応 |
| Modulator | Residual PPO (計画) | TRI DPPO, NVIDIA Eureka | DPPO検討価値あり | Phase D で比較 |
| Safety Envelope | action clip + joint limits | MIT HOCBF + Stanford HJ | **最大GAP** | HOCBF導入 (Phase B) |
| NC/PS Interface | action制限+Phase限定+freeze+抽象API | ETH RSL teacher-student | 対等 | — |

### 参考: 対立するE2Eアプローチ

DeepMind Gemini Robotics, Figure AI Helix, Physical Intelligence は全てを学習するE2E VLAを採用。
THREADの「構造が先、変調が後」は少数派だが、以下で優位:
- **安全性**: E2E VLAはCBF/Energy Tank未実装。意味的安全のみ
- **解釈可能性**: Phase FSMにより何が起きているか追跡可能
- **sim-to-real信頼性**: 工学構造はドメインギャップに頑健（ETH RSL実績）

| R0 | Lift高さ拡張 (10mm→50mm) | ✅ 3段階全PASS (20/35/50mm)、cable_z_delta=53mm |
| R1a | 水平移動 50mm | ✅ 3/3 PASS、xy_err=4.5mm |
| R1b | フルRoute 131mm | ✅ 3/3 PASS（Fix1+Fix2適用後）、xy_err=7.67mm |
| R2a | Arm Separation | ✅ 3/3 PASS、ee_err L=3.1mm R=3.5mm |
| R2b | Ascend (Z=1.09) | ✅ 3/3 PASS（speed半減で収束確保）、ee_err R=18.7mm |
| R2c | Descent + Drape | ✅ 3/3 PASS、cable physics再有効化NaN=0 |
| R2d | Release | ✅ 3/3 PASS、cable_on_hook=true、cable_z=0.755 |
